<tr><td><a id="row_1513_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__image__confirmed.html">img_mgmt_image_confirmed</a></td><td><span data-translation="1021">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_DFU_CONFIRMED通知回调中提供的结构。该回调用于通知应用映像确认（Image Confirmation）已成功执行。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1021">Structure provided in the <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/group__mcumgr__callback__api__img__mgmt.html#gga3fa2c42bade08cca1122e32f6315f107a36e486e194d0d6013d188b4ed70a95fb">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_DFU_CONFIRMED</a> notification callback: This callback function is used to notify the application about an image confirmation being executed successfully </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1514_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__slot__info__image.html">img_mgmt_slot_info_image</a></td><td><span data-translation="1022">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_SLOT_INFO_IMAGE通知回调中提供的结构。使用槽位信息命令（Slot Info Command）时，每个映像调用该回调一次，可借此在响应中返回附加信息或字段。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1022">Structure provided in the <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/group__mcumgr__callback__api__img__mgmt.html#gga3fa2c42bade08cca1122e32f6315f107acd93051e050127a961843e3a8650fda3">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_SLOT_INFO_IMAGE</a> notification callback: This callback function is called once per image when the slot info command is used, it can be used to return additional information/fields in the response </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1515_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__slot__info__slot.html">img_mgmt_slot_info_slot</a></td><td><span data-translation="1023">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_SLOT_INFO_SLOT通知回调中提供的结构。使用槽位信息命令（Slot Info Command）时，每个映像的每个槽位调用该回调一次，可借此在响应中返回附加信息或字段。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1023">Structure provided in the <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/group__mcumgr__callback__api__img__mgmt.html#gga3fa2c42bade08cca1122e32f6315f107a9e1b6d2ae97ef43edc7ad4749b7ac9b0">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_SLOT_INFO_SLOT</a> notification callback: This callback function is called once per slot per image when the slot info command is used, it can be used to return additional information/fields in the response </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1516_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__state.html">img_mgmt_state</a></td><td><span data-translation="1024">进行中的上传所用的全局状态（Global State）。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1024">Global state for upload in progress </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1517_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__state__slot__encode.html">img_mgmt_state_slot_encode</a></td><td><span data-translation="1025">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_IMAGE_SLOT_STATE通知回调中提供的结构。该回调允许应用或模块向映像槽位状态（Image Slot State）响应追加自定义字段。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1025">Structure provided in the <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/group__mcumgr__callback__api__img__mgmt.html#gga3fa2c42bade08cca1122e32f6315f107a52a1b8f3d4dae7bab74fd6187500acc7">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_IMAGE_SLOT_STATE</a> notification callback: This callback function is used to allow applications or modules append custom fields to the image slot state response </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1518_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__upload__action.html">img_mgmt_upload_action</a></td><td><span data-translation="1026">描述处理上传请求（Upload Request）时要执行的操作。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1026">Describes what to do during processing of an upload request </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1519_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__upload__check.html">img_mgmt_upload_check</a></td><td><span data-translation="1027">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_DFU_CHUNK通知回调中提供的结构。该回调用于将来自客户端的待处理固件上传包通知应用，并授权或拒绝该上传。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1027">Structure provided in the <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/group__mcumgr__callback__api__img__mgmt.html#gga3fa2c42bade08cca1122e32f6315f107a19d53f0c0f6649858e97fbcecfbaf24c">MGMT_EVT_OP_IMG_MGMT_DFU_CHUNK</a> notification callback: This callback function is used to notify the application about a pending firmware upload packet from a client and authorise or deny it </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1520_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structimg__mgmt__upload__req.html">img_mgmt_upload_req</a></td><td><span data-translation="1028">表示单个上传请求（Upload Request）。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1028">Represents an individual upload request </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1955_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__linkaddr.html">net_linkaddr</a></td><td><span data-translation="1397">硬件链路地址（Hardware Link Address）结构。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1397">Hardware link address structure </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1956_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__lldp__chassis__tlv.html">net_lldp_chassis_tlv</a></td><td><span data-translation="1398">机箱ID类型-长度-值（Chassis ID TLV），见IEEE 802.1AB第8.5.2节。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1398">Chassis ID TLV, see chapter 8.5.2 in IEEE 802.1AB </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1957_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__lldp__port__tlv.html">net_lldp_port_tlv</a></td><td><span data-translation="1399">端口ID类型-长度-值（Port ID TLV），见IEEE 802.1AB第8.5.3节。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1399">Port ID TLV, see chapter 8.5.3 in IEEE 802.1AB </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1958_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__lldp__time__to__live__tlv.html">net_lldp_time_to_live_tlv</a></td><td><span data-translation="1400">生存时间类型-长度-值（Time To Live TLV），见IEEE 802.1AB第8.5.4节。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1400">Time To Live TLV, see chapter 8.5.4 in IEEE 802.1AB </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1960_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__mgmt__event__callback.html">net_mgmt_event_callback</a></td><td><span data-translation="1402">网络管理事件回调（Network Management Event Callback）结构；用于向网络管理事件部分注册回调，使该结构的所有者能够根据给定事件掩码接收网络事件通知。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1402">Network Management event callback structure Used to register a callback into the network management event part, in order to let the owner of this struct to get network event notification based on given event mask </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1961_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__mgmt__msghdr.html">net_mgmt_msghdr</a></td><td><span data-translation="1403">每条网络管理消息（Network Management Message）都以前置的此头部开始。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1403">Each network management message is prefixed with this header </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1964_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__pkt.html">net_pkt</a></td><td><span data-translation="1406">网络数据包（Network Packet）。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1406">Network packet </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1968_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__sockaddr__can.html">net_sockaddr_can</a></td><td><span data-translation="1410">struct net_sockaddr_can：CAN套接字（Socket）使用的net_sockaddr结构。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1410">Struct <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__sockaddr__can.html">net_sockaddr_can</a> - The <a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__sockaddr.html">net_sockaddr</a> structure for CAN sockets </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1975_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__stats.html">net_stats</a></td><td><span data-translation="1417">在一个结构中保存全部网络统计信息（Network Statistics）。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1417">All network statistics in one struct </small></td></tr>

<tr><td><a id="row_1978_"></a><span style="width:16px;display:inline-block;"> </span><span><span>C</span></span><a href="https://docs.zephyrproject.org/latest/doxygen/html/structnet__stats__eth.html">net_stats_eth</a></td><td><span data-translation="1420">全部以太网（Ethernet）专用统计信息。</span><br><small lang="en" data-source-prose="1420">All Ethernet specific statistics </small></td></tr>
